ABOUT FORTUNE REIT 有關置富產業信託
Established in 2003, Fortune Real Estate Investment Trust (“Fortune         置富產業信託（「置富產業信託」）於 2003 年成立，
REIT”) is a real estate investment trust constituted by a trust deed (the   是一家由信託契約（經不時修訂、補充或以其他
“Trust Deed”) (as amended, supplemented or otherwise modified               方式修改）（「信託契約」）所組成的房地產投資信
from time to time). It is the first REIT to hold assets in Hong Kong and    託基金。其為首家持有香港資產的房地產投資信
is currently listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong         託基金，現於香港聯合交易所有限公司（「香港聯
Kong Limited (the “SEHK”).                                                  交所」）主板上市。

Fortune REIT holds a portfolio of 17 retail properties, with 16 private     置富產業信託持有 17 個零售物業（包括於香港的
housing estate retail properties in Hong Kong and 1 neighbourhood           16 個私人住宅屋苑零售物業及於新加坡的 1 個社
mall in Singapore, comprising 3 million square feet (“sq.ft.”) of retail    區 商 場），物 業 組 合 包 括 約 300 萬 平 方 呎（「平 方
space and 2,793 car parking spaces.                                         呎」）零售空間及 2,793 個車位。

to holders of Fortune REIT units (“Unitholders”) through proactive          旗下的資產組合及收購可為置富產業信託的基金

    Chairman’s Report
    主席報告

                                                      Dear Unitholders,

                                                      On behalf of the board (the “Board”) of directors
                                                      (the “Director(s)”), I am pleased to present the
                                                      annual report of Fortune REIT for the financial
                                                      year ended 31 December 2025 (“FY2025” or the
                                                      “Reporting Year”) and to share its outlook for
                                                      2026.

                                                      Hong Kong’s economy gained further momentum
                                                      in 2025, with GDP growth accelerating to 3.5%
                                                      from 2.4% in 2024. The retail market continued
                                                      its gradual recovery since mid-2025, though
                                                      progress remained uneven as the city contented
                                                      with structure shifts associated with a broader
                                                      economic transformation. The rise of the new
                                                      economy, alongside increased outbound travel
                                                      and intensifying online competition, continued to
                                                      reshape consumer behavior.


                                                      致基金單位持有人：

                                                      本人謹代表董事（「董事」）會（「董事會」），欣然
                                                      提呈置富產業信託截至 2025 年 12 月 31 日止財
                                                      政 年 度（「2025 年 財 政 年 度」或「報 告 年 度」）的
                                                      年報並分享對 2026 年的展望。

                                                      2025 年，香港經濟動力進一步加強，本地生產
                                                      總值增長由 2024 年的 2.4% 加快至 3.5%。零售
                                                      市道自 2025 年中起持續穩步復甦，惟在本港宏
                                                      觀經濟邁向結構性轉型之際，復甦步伐依然快
                                                      慢不一。新經濟興起，加上外遊增加及網購競
                                                      爭加劇，正重塑消費者行為。




    Chui Sing Loi (alias Tsui Sing Loi)
    徐勝來
    Chairman and Independent Non-Executive Director
    主席兼獨立非執行董事

FY2025 RESULTS OVERVIEW                                               2025 年財政年度業績概覽

Fortune REIT recorded revenue of HK$1,682.4 million and net           於 2025 財政年度，置富產業信託錄得收益 1,682.4 百
property income of HK$1,188.1 million for FY2025, representing        萬港元及物業收入淨額 1,188.1 百萬港元，分別按年
year-on-year declines of 3.7% and 5.2% respectively, primarily        減少 3.7% 及 5.2%，主要由於租金調升率錄得負增長
due to negative rental reversion. Operating expenses rose             所致。營運開支雖略有上升，惟於下半年（「2025 年
modestly but stabilized in the second half of the year (“2H2025”).    下半年」）已見回穩。

A notable highlight was the 14.4% reduction in finance costs as       值得注意的是，融資成本隨香港銀行同業拆息回落
HIBOR trended lower, which helped cushion rental pressure.            而 下 降 14.4%，有 助 緩 解 租 金 下 調 帶 來 的 壓 力。因
Consequently, income available for distribution remained steady       此，可供分派收入維持穩定，為 722.9 百萬港元。董
at HK$722.9 million. The Board is pleased to maintain a 100%          事會欣然報告，派息比率維持 100%，2025 財政年度
dividend payout ratio, resulting in a distribution per unit (“DPU”)   每基金單位分派（「每基金單位分派」）為 35.22 港仙。
of 35.22 HK cents for FY2025 and yielding 7.2% based on the           按 基 金 單 位 於 2025 年 12 月 31 日 之 收 市 價 4.89 港 元

TRANSFORMING FORTUNE MALLS INTO VIBRANT,                              將置富 MALLS 打造成以體驗為本的活力社區樞紐
EXPERIENCE-LED COMMUNITY HUBS

In a rapidly evolving retail market, Fortune REIT remained focused    在零售市場急速演變之際，置富產業信託始終專注
on strengthening portfolio fundamentals. Our proactive and flexible   於強化物業組合基本面。我們採取積極靈活的租賃
leasing strategy helped maintain strong, long-term relationships      策略，與優質租戶維持穩固的長期合作關係，帶動
with quality tenants, resulting in a higher occupancy of 95.8% and    出租率攀升至 95.8%，續租率亦高達 83%。
a strong retention rate of 83%.

Consumer behaviour is undergoing a structural shift, influenced       由於來自中國內地的競爭加劇、網上平台興起及生
by intensified competition from the Chinese Mainland, the rise        活習慣改變，消費者行為正發生結構性轉變。現今
of online platforms, and evolving lifestyle patterns. Increasingly,   消費者除了重視便利性外，也希望在休閒空間中獲
shoppers are looking for destinations that offer not only             得社交互動、娛樂體驗和社區聯繫。商場由純粹的
convenience but also social interaction, leisure, and a sense of      購物場所轉型為聚會與體驗於一身的樞紐。我們因
community. As malls transit from purely transactional spaces          此制定明確策略，持續優化資產。
to hubs for gathering and experience, we deepened our asset
optimization efforts with clear priorities.

     A more welcoming entrance with a bold, modern statement that improves first impressions for visitors.
     以鮮明的現代設計為大門口營造更友善的氛圍，為顧客留下更佳的第一印象。




     Waldorf Avenue 華都大道

     The HK$9 million AEIs for Waldorf Avenue, which commenced in February 2025, were successfully completed in July 2025.
     This comprehensive transformation has revitalized the avenue with a refreshed and appealing brand image, as well as
     enhanced facilities, driving stronger footfall and attracting a broader range of tenants spanning daily necessities, F&B, and
     convenience services.

     Key enhancements include a refurbished grand entrance, energy-efficient LED ceiling illumination, newly configured
     feature walls with playful design elements, new LED displays, and a redesigned self-service Gift Redemption Counter — all
     contributing to better retail shopfronts and a more vibrant, welcoming shopping environment.

     With these proactive improvements, Waldorf Avenue strengthens its position as the leading community retail destination in
     Tuen Mun.

     耗資 9 百萬港元的華都大道資產增值措施於 2025 年 2 月展開，並已於 2025 年 7 月順利完工。該全面翻新工程賦予華都
     大道煥然一新、卓爾不群的品牌形象，同時提升了設施，不僅帶動更多人流，並吸引了更多行業租戶進駐，涵蓋日用品、
     餐飲及便利服務等各個行業。

     主要升級工程包括翻新大門口、裝設節能 LED 天花板照明、以富趣味的設計元素重新配置特色牆、裝設新 LED 顯示屏
     及重新設計自助禮品換領櫃檯，全部均有助促進提升零售店面形象及營造更熱鬧、更友善的購物環境。

     華都大道憑藉主動進行上述改善工程，鞏固了其作為屯門領先社區零售熱點的地位。

     Laguna Plaza 麗港城商場

     The HK$32 million AEIs for Laguna Plaza, which commenced in February 2025, were completed in December 2025. The
     strategic upgrade transformed over 26,000 sq.ft. of basement retail space, including right-sizing the supermarket to unlock
     opportunities for a more diverse and appealing retail and F&B mix. Upon completion, the refreshed space welcomed a new
     restaurant, a beauty salon, and a lifestyle store, revitalizing Laguna Plaza as a vibrant community hub.

     耗資 3,200 萬港元的麗港城商場資產增值措施於 2025 年 2 月展開，並已於 2025 年 12 月完成。該策略升級工程改造了逾
     26,000 平方呎的地庫零售空間，包括調整超級市場的面積以騰出更多空間，為更多元化且更具吸引力的零售企業及餐
     飲食肆締造商機。翻新後麗港城商場煥然一新，迎來了一間新食肆、一間美容院及一間生活百貨專門店，成為綻放活
     力的社區樞紐。

     FORTUNE CITY ONE 置富第一城

     Fortune City One, located in Shatin, New Territories and
     south of the Shing Mun River, is the commercial constituent
     of the 10,642-unit City One Shatin residential development.
     The City One Shatin residential development was
     completed in the 1980s and is one of the largest residential
     developments in Hong Kong. Fortune City One serves the
                                                                    1
     daily needs of the local City One Shatin residents, as well
     as residents from other developments in the vicinity. The
     catchment population consists of young families, students
     and workers in the area.

     Fortune City One can be easily accessed by private and
     public transportation. It is strategically located near City
     One MTR Station and in close proximity to the Sha Tin MTR
     Station.

     置 富 第 一 城 位 於 新 界 沙 田，坐 落 城 門 河 以 南，乃 提 供
     10,642 個單位的沙田第一城住宅發展項目之商業組成部
     分。沙田第一城住宅發展項目於 1980 年代落成，為香港
     最大型的住宅發展項目之一。置富第一城為沙田第一城
     的住戶及周邊地區其他發展項目的居民提供日常必需品。
     服務對象包括該區的年輕家庭、學生及在職人士。

     置富第一城交通方便，可乘私人及公共交通工具前往。
     該物業策略性地位於第一城港鐵站附近，並鄰近沙田港
     鐵站。

     +WOO 嘉湖

     +WOO is located in Tin Shui Wai, Yuen Long, New
     Territories, one of the biggest new towns in Hong Kong.
     Being the largest shopping center in Tin Shui Wai, the
     property serves a catchment of close to 288,000 people,
     the hotel residents of the 1,102-room Harbour Plaza Resort
     City, as well as visitors from Mainland China due to its close
                                                                      2
     proximity to the border. +WOO is located only 15 minutes
     away from the Wetland Park via Light Rail Transit, which is
     a key tourist spot for both local and overseas visitors.

     +WOO 嘉湖位於新界元朗天水圍，天水圍為香港最大的
     新市鎮之一。+WOO 嘉湖為天水圍區最大的購物中心。
     該 物 業 主 要 服 務 區 內 近 288,000 人 口 及 嘉 湖海逸酒店共
     1,102 房間之住客，並因毗鄰內地邊境而同時服務中國內
     地遊客。乘坐輕鐵從 +WOO 嘉湖前往本地及海外遊客的
     熱門觀光點濕地公園僅需 15 分鐘。

     MA ON SHAN PLAZA 馬鞍山廣場

     Ma On Shan Plaza is located in Ma On Shan, Shatin, New
     Territories, which is a well-established new town, and home
     to about 206,000 people.

     Ma On Shan Plaza is directly connected to Ma On
     Shan MTR Station and is easily accessible by public
                                                                      3
     transportation with bus terminals, a public light bus
     terminal, and a taxi waiting area. Ma On Shan Plaza is part
     of the 1,102-unit Bayshore Towers residential complex and
     is linked to the adjacent 4,760-unit Sunshine City residential
     development, as well as the 5.5-hectare Ma On Shan Park,
     by a number of covered footbridges.

     馬鞍山廣場位於新界沙田馬鞍山，馬鞍山為一個發展完
     備的新市鎮，人口約 20.6 萬。

     馬鞍山廣場與馬鞍山港鐵站直接相連，毗鄰巴士總站、
     公共小巴總站及的士站，交通十分便捷。馬鞍山廣場為
     提供 1,102 個單位的海栢花園住宅綜合項目的一部分，與
     附近提供 4,760 個單位的新港城住宅發展項目，以及面積
     達 5.5 公頃的馬鞍山公園以多條有蓋行人天橋相連接。

        a corporate governance framework with internal controls, systems, policies and procedures in place to promote the
        operation of Fortune REIT in an accountable and transparent manner and with built-in checks and balances to ensure
        compliance with relevant regulations.
        為建立及維持高水準企業管治的目標，董事會已建立企業管治架構，且已制定內部監控、系統、政策及程序，
        以促使置富產業信託以問責及具透明度的方式營運，並輔以內部監察及制衡，藉以確保遵守相關規例。

BOARD EVALUATION                                                     董事會評核

During the Reporting Year, an internal Board Review was              董事會於報告年度進行了一次內部董事會檢視，採
conducted in the form of a Board evaluation. The process             取董事會評核形式。評核過程要求每位董事填寫網
involved each Director completing an online evaluation covering a    上評核表格，涵蓋董事會組成及架構、董事會會議
broad range of topics including Board composition and structure,     安 排、策 略 監 督、培 訓 及 資 源 等 廣 泛 議 題。經 分 析
Board meeting logistics, strategic oversight and training and        董事提供的寶貴意見及評價後，已按需要制定改進
resources. The valuable feedback and comments from Directors         計劃。基於評核結果及提名委員會持續進行的檢討，
were analysed and improvement plans have been drawn up               董事會認為其於整年內已維持適當水平的有效運作。
as necessary. Based on the findings of the evaluation and the
ongoing reviews conducted by the Nomination Committee, the
Board considered that its effectiveness was maintained at an
appropriate level throughout the year.

      Board 董事會

      The Board is vested with the overall responsibility for corporate governance, business operations and risks, ESG, financial
      performance and the appointment of Directors. The Board provides overall oversight and leadership, driving the strategic
      direction of Fortune REIT and the Manager, including acquisitions and key corporate actions.
      董事會獲賦予企業管治、業務運營及風險、環境、社會及管治、財務表現及委任董事的整體責任。董事會提供整體
      監督及領導、推動置富產業信託及管理人的戰略導向（包括收購及關鍵企業行動）。

      Reserved Matters for the Board 保留予董事會的事項

      There are certain matters which are reserved for the Board to approve including but not limited to:
      若干事項保留予董事會，以批准，包括但不限於：

      •     the interim and final distribution of Fortune REIT
            置富產業信託的中期及末期分派
      •     the annual and interim reports and accounts and Circulars to Unitholders
            致基金單位持有人之年度及中期報告及賬目以及通函
      •     changes to the constitutional documents of the Manager and the Corporate Governance Policy
            管理人章程文件及企業管治政策之變動
      •     appointment and removal of Directors
            任免董事
      •     recommending Unitholders to approve changes to the provisions of the Trust Deed
            建議基金單位持有人批准修訂信託契約條文
      •     recommending to Unitholders to note the appointment and removal of auditors of Fortune REIT and the approval of
            the audit fee
            建議基金單位持有人知悉置富產業信託核數師之任免並批准審計費用
      •     treasury, taxes, financial risk management and capital policies and significant changes in accounting policy
            財務、稅務、金融風險管理及資本政策以及會計政策的重大變動
      •     acquisitions and disposals of properties which exceeds 15% of the gross asset value of Fortune REIT
            收購及處置超過置富產業信託資產總值 15% 之物業
      •     the issue of new units
            發行新基金單位
      •     capital expenditure, acquisitions, and disposals in excess of the discretionary power of the relevant officer (if any) of
            the Manager
            資本開支、收購及處置超過管理人相關工作人員（如有）酌情範圍之事宜
      •     any matter which would have a material effect on Fortune REIT’s financial position, liabilities, future strategy or
            reputation
            任何對置富產業信託財務狀況、負債、未來戰略或聲譽產生重大影響之事項
      •     material contracts not in the ordinary course of business
            非日常業務過程中的重大合約
      •     power and authority of Board-appointed committees
            委任董事會委員會之權利及權限
      •     potential areas of conflict
            潛在衝突範圍

      The Board has delegated its power to the Audit Committee, the Disclosures Committee, the Designated Committee and
      the Nomination Committee to assist with the review of specific issues. Each of the Audit Committee, the Disclosures
      Committee, the Designated Committee and the Nomination Committee has been set up with clear terms of reference and
      report back to the Board on their findings and recommendations.
      董事會已授權審核委員會、披露委員會、專責委員會及提名委員會協助審查具體事宜。審核委員會、披露委員會、
      專責委員會及提名委員會各自均有明確的職權範圍，並向董事會匯報其調查結果及建議。

     WHISTLEBLOWING AND ANTI-CORRUPTION POLICIES                            舉報及反貪污政策

     The Manager is committed to maintaining high standards of              管理人致力於保持高標準的商業道德及透明度，並
     business ethics and transparency, and considers effective              認為有效的合規政策、程序及流程是良好企業管治
     compliance policies, procedures and processes as essential             的基本要素。
     elements of good corporate governance.

     The Audit Committee, as delegated by the Board, has put in             根據董事會授權，審核委員會設有舉報政策，該政
     place a whistleblowing policy, which extends to persons other          策擴大至適用於除管理人的僱員以外的所有人士。
     than employees of the Manager. Under this policy, the Audit            根據上述政策，審核委員會負責檢討管理人的僱員
     Committee reviews arrangements by which employees of the               及任何其他人士可就財務報告或其他事宜可能存在
     Manager and any other persons may, in confidence, raise                的缺失以保密方式提出關注的安排。該政策為管理
     concerns about possible improprieties in matters of financial          人的所有員工及就管理人可能存在的不當及不利行
     reporting or other matters. The policy provides a communication        動以保密方式提出關注的任何其他人士提供交流渠
     channel for all employees of the Manager, as well as any other         道。該政策亦保護舉報者免因公正無詐地舉報而遭
     persons, who may in confidence raise concerns about possible           受報復或迫害。該政策的目的是確保可就關注事宜
     improprieties and obstructive action within the Manager. The           進行獨立調查並就適當的跟進行動作出安排。
     policy also protects complainants from reprisals or victimization
     when they whistle blow in good faith and without malice. The
     objective is to ensure that arrangements are in place for the
     independent investigation of such matters and for appropriate
     follow-up action.

      MATTERS TO BE DECIDED BY UNITHOLDERS BY                                 須由基金單位持有人以特別決議案決定的事項
      EXTRAORDINARY RESOLUTION

      Under the Trust Deed, decisions with respect to certain matters         根據信託契約，與若干事項有關的決定須事先以特
      require specific prior approval of the Unitholders by way of            別決議案獲得基金單位持有人特別批准。該等事項
      extraordinary resolution. Such matters include: (a) disposal            包括：(a) 於收購置富產業信託土地後兩年內出售構
      of any land or an interest, option or right over any of the land        成置富產業信託資產一部分的任何土地或任何土地
      forming part of the assets of Fortune REIT or shares in any             的權益、選擇權或權利或持有置富產業信託的該等
      property company holding such land, option or right over any of         土地或任何土地的選擇權或權利的任何物業公司的
      the land for Fortune REIT within two years of the acquisition of        股份；(b) 管理人管理費費率任何超出獲准限額的增
      such land; (b) any increase in the rate above the permitted limit       幅或結構的變動；(c) 受託人費用率任何超出獲准限
      or change in structure of the Manager’s management fees; (c)            額的增幅或結構的變動；(d) 信託契約的若干修改；
      any increase in the rate above the permitted limit or change in         (e) 終止置富產業信託（惟不包括若干有限的情況）；(f)
      structure of the Trustee’s fees; (d) certain modifications to the       置富產業信託進行合併；(g) 罷免外部核數師；(h) 罷
      Trust Deed; (e) termination of Fortune REIT (except in certain          免受託人；及 (i) 置富產業信託的投資政策變動。
      limited circumstances); (f) merger of Fortune REIT; (g) removal of
      the external Auditor; (h) removal of the Trustee; and (i) a change in
      Fortune REIT’s investment policy.

      Any decisions to be made by resolution of the Unitholders other         任何除上文事宜以外透過基金單位持有人的決議案
      than the above shall be made by ordinary resolution, unless an          作出的決定，均須以普通決議案提呈，除非是按適
      extraordinary resolution is required by the Applicable Rules.           用法規須以特別決議案提呈。

      BASIS OF VALUATION                                                     估值基準

      Unless otherwise stated, our valuation has been prepared in             除另有說明外，吾等的估值乃根據香港測量師學會
      accordance with the “HKIS Valuation Standards 2024 Edition”            （「香港測量師學會」）頒佈的《香港測量師學會估值準
      published by The Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors (“HKIS”), the         則（2024 年版）》、國際估值準則委員會（「國際估值
      “International Valuation Standards” published by the International      準則委員會」）頒佈的《國際估值準則》及皇家特許測
      Valuation Standards Council (“IVSC”) and the “RICS Valuation —          量師學會（「皇家特許測量師學會」）頒佈的《皇家特許
      Global Standards” published by the Royal Institution of Chartered       測量師學會估值 — 全球準則》編製，並可作出變動，
      Surveyors (“RICS”) subject to variation to meet local established       以配合本地已制定之法律。除另有說明外，吾等作
      law. Unless otherwise stated, our valuations are undertaken as          為相關估值準則所界定的外聘估值師進行估值。
      External Valuers as defined in the relevant Valuation Standards.

      Our valuation is made on the basis of Market Value as defined by       吾等的估值乃根據國際估值準則委員會和新加坡測
      IVSC and SISV Valuation Standards and Practice Guidelines and          量師與估價師估值準則及實務指引所界定以及香港
      adopted by HKIS and RICS, set out as:                                  測量師學會及皇家特許測量師學會所採納的市值基
                                                                             準進行，載列為：

      “the estimated amount for which an asset or liability should           「在進行適當市場推廣後，自願買方及自願賣方雙方
      exchange on the valuation date between a willing buyer and a           按公平原則並在知情、審慎及非強逼的情況下於估
      willing seller in an arm’s-length transaction after proper marketing   值日進行交易的估計資產或負債交易款額。」
      and where the parties had each acted knowledgeably, prudently
      and without compulsion.”

      Our valuation presented in the report would represent 100%             吾等於本報告呈列的估值指物業組合的 100% 權益，
      interest of the Portfolio and not the share holdings of the company    而非指持有物業組合權益之公司的股權。
      holding the Portfolio interest hereof.

      VALUATION METHODOLOGIES                                                估值方法

      We have made use of the Income Capitalisation Method cross-            吾等已採用收益資本化法，並參照直接比較法。
      referenced with the Direct Comparison Method.

      Income Capitalisation Method                                           收益資本化法收益

      The income capitalisation method is based on the capitalisation        資本化法乃按照從估值日起物業組合餘下租期內的
      of the current passing rental income and potential reversionary        現時租金收入及潛在復歸收入以適當的投資收益率
      income over the remaining tenure of the Portfolio from the date        進 行 資 本 化，以 達 致 資 本 值。對 於 免 租 期、持 續 空
      of valuation at appropriate investment yields to arrive at the         置╱推廣期及空置空間的不可收回支出，估值時已
      capital value. The appropriate adjustments/deductions for rent         作適當調整╱扣減。
      free period, ongoing vacancy voids/marketing periods and non
      recoverable expenses for the vacant space have been allowed.

      The income capitalisation method can more accurately reflect           收益資本化法透過分析市場現象而作出各種具體假
      these property specific factors by utilising various specific          設，運 用 時 更 能 準 確 反 映 該 等 物 業 的 特 性。迄 今，
      assumptions which have been derived via analysis of market             資本化法中應用該等假設的能力更適合於對投資者
      evidence. The ability to apply these assumptions in the                著重回報的投資物業進行估值。
      capitalisation method is by far more appropriate for valuing an
      investment property where investors’ emphasis on delivering
      returns is of paramount importance.

Direct Comparison Method                                                 直接比較法

The direct comparison method is the most widely used method              直接比較法為香港最廣泛運用的估值方法，乃基於
of valuation in Hong Kong and is based on comparing the                  將予估值的物業與其他可資比較物業（近期轉手或出
properties to be valued directly with other comparable properties        租）直接比較。該等物業一般位於周邊地區或有關物
which recently changed hands or leased. These premises are               業的其他可資比較市場。然而，由於房地產物業的
generally located in the surrounding areas or in another market          特殊性質，通常需作出適當調整，以顧及可能影響
which is comparable to the properties. However, because of               考量中物業或會達至的價格╱租金的任何定性與定
the heterogeneous nature of real estate properties, appropriate          量差異。
adjustments are usually required to allow for any qualitative and
quantitative differences that may affect the price/rental likely to be
achieved by the properties under consideration.

Factors such as tenants covenants, trade mix are difficult               租賃契約及租戶組合等因素在可資比較物業的整體
to be quantified in the overall unit value of the comparables.           單位價值中難以量化。另外，市場上未必有恰當的
Furthermore, good comparables may not be readily available               可資比較物業。鑒於物業組合的特性，吾等因而更
in the market. In the light of the characteristics of the Portfolio,     加依賴收益資本化法以得出估值結論。
we have therefore placed more weighting on the reliance on
the income capitalisation method in arriving at our valuation
conclusion.

Valuation Reconciliation                                                 估值綜論

The results of the two valuation methods will be reconciled and          兩種估值方法的結果將予以綜合，而評估價值將按
the assessed value will be analysed in terms of estimated net            照估計淨物業收益率進行分析。
property yield.

and air-conditioning charges) of all lettable units of each             評估管理人及估值師所作判斷的合適性，尤其是
property are capitalised for the respective unexpired terms             基於對香港及新加坡物業市場了解所採用的估值

收益資本化法作出之估值為基準。收益資本化法是一種估
值方法，透過此方法，每項物業內所有可出租單位的現有
租金收入淨額（即不包括差餉、政府地租、管理費及空調費）
就各自的未到期租約期資本化進行，並假設現有租賃合約
到期時以各自的市值租金出租，而空置單位則假設按估值
日的市值租金出租。此乃涉及參考分析香港及新加坡同類
物業銷售交易所得收益，並根據估值師對物業投資者市場
預期的認知作出調整以反映 貴集團投資物業的相關因素
後，作出若干假設及使用有關資本化利率的估計。

      RESPONSIBILITIES OF MANAGER AND THOSE CHARGED                          管理人及負責綜合財務報表管治人士之責任
      WITH GOVERNANCE FOR THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL
      STATEMENTS

      The Manager is responsible for the preparation of the consolidated     管理人負責根據國際會計準則理事會頒佈的國際財
      financial statements that give a true and fair view in accordance      務報告準則編製綜合財務報表，以提供真實而公平
      with IFRS Accounting Standards as issued by the IASB, and for          的反映，並須負責管理人認為就編製綜合財務報表
      such internal control as the Manager determines is necessary           而言屬必要的內部監控，以使財務報表不存在因欺
      to enable the preparation of consolidated financial statements         詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述。
      that are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or
      error.

      In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the Manager        於編製綜合財務報表時，管理人須負責評估 貴集
      is responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a      團持續經營之能力，並於適用情況下披露與持續經
      going concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters related to going     營相關的事宜，以及使用持續經營會計基準，惟管
      concern and using the going concern basis of accounting unless         理人擬對 貴集團進行清盤或停止營業，或別無其
      the Manager either intends to liquidate the Group or to cease          他實際替代方案除外。
      operations, or has no realistic alternative but to do so.

3   BASIS OF PREPARATION OF CONSOLIDATED                            3     綜合財務報表的編製基準及主要會計政策資料
    FINANCIAL STATEMENTS AND MATERIAL
    ACCOUNTING POLICY INFORMATION

    3.1   Basis of preparation of consolidated financial                  3.1   綜合財務報表的編製基準
          statements

          The consolidated financial statements have been                       綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事
          prepared in accordance with IFRS Accounting                           會頒佈的國際財務報告準則會計準則編
          Standards as issued by the IASB. For the purpose of                   製。就 編 製 綜 合 財 務 報 表 而 言，倘 可 合
          preparation of the consolidated financial statements,                 理預期資料將影響主要使用者作出的決
          information is considered material if such information                定，則該資料視作重大資料。此外，綜合
          is reasonably expected to influence decisions made by                 財務報表包含香港聯交所證券上市規則
          primary users. In addition, the consolidated financial                規 定 的 適 用 披 露、信 託 契 約 的 相 關 條 文
          statements include applicable disclosures required by                 及證監會頒佈的房地產投資信託基金守則。
          the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the
          SEHK, the relevant provisions of the Trust Deed and
          the REIT Code as issued by the SFC.

          The consolidated financial statements have been                       除於每一個報告期末按公平值計量的投
          prepared on historical cost basis, except for                         資 物 業 及 若 干 金 融 工 具 外，綜 合 財 務 報
          investment properties and certain financial instruments               表乃按歷史成本基準編製（如下文所載會
          that are measured at fair values at the end of each                   計政策所闡釋）。
          reporting period, as explained in the accounting
          policies set out below.

          Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of               歷史成本一般根據交換貨品及服務時所
          the consideration given in exchange for goods and                     給予代價的公平值計算。
          services.

          The Manager is of the opinion that, taking into                       管 理 人 認 為，考 慮 到 為 取 得 額 外 銀 行 融
          account the headroom of the fair value of investment                  資 之 投 資 物 業 公 平 值 之 空 間、本 集 團 之
          properties for obtaining additional banking facilities,               內部財務資源及現時可動用之未提取的
          internal financial resources of the Group and presently               銀 行 融 資，本 集 團 擁 有 足 夠 營 運 資 金 應
          available undrawn banking facilities, the Group has                   付由報告期間完結起計一年內之目前需
          sufficient working capital for its present requirements               要。因 此，綜 合 財 務 報 表 已 按 持 續 經 營
          within one year from the end of the reporting period.                 之基準編製。
          Hence, the consolidated financial statements have
          been prepared on a going concern basis.

                       The consolidated financial statements                                 綜合財務報表包括置富產業信託及

                       Fortune REIT:                                                         權：

                            returns from its involvement with the                                或有權獲得可變回報；及
                            investee; and

                            returns.

                       indicate that there are changes to one or more                        富產業信託將重新評估其是否對被
                       of the three elements of control listed above.                        投資方擁有控制權。

                       Fortune REIT obtains control over the subsidiary                      業信託獲得該附屬公司的控制權時
                       and ceases when Fortune REIT loses control of                         開始，並於置富產業信託失去該附
                       the subsidiary.                                                       屬公司之控制權時終止。

          (b)   Investment properties

                Investment properties are properties held to                               投資物業為持有以賺取租金及╱或
                earn rentals and/or for capital appreciation.                              資本升值的物業。

                Investment properties are initially measured                               投 資 物 業 乃 首 次 以 成 本（包 括 任 何
                at cost, including any directly attributable                               直 接 應 佔 開 支）計 量。在 初 始 確 認
                expenditure. Subsequent to initial recognition,                            後，投 資 物 業 乃 按 公 平 值 計 量，經
                investment properties are measured at fair value,                          調整以撇除任何預付或應計經營租
                adjusted to exclude any prepaid or accrued                                 賃收入。
                operating lease income.

                Gains and losses arising from changes in the fair                          投資物業公平值變動所產生的收益
                value of investment properties are included in                             及虧損乃於產生期內計入損益。
                profit or loss for the period in which they arise.

          (c)   Financial instruments                                               (c)    金融工具

                Financial assets and financial liabilities are                             金融資產及金融負債乃於集團實體
                recognised when a group entity becomes                                     成為工具的合約條款一方時確認。
                a party to the contractual provisions of the
                instrument.

                Financial assets and financial liabilities are                             金融資產及金融負債最初按公平值
                initially measured at fair value except for                                計量，惟客戶合約產生的貿易應收
                trade receivables arising from contracts                                   款項最初根據國際財務報告準則第
                with customers which are initially measured                                15 號客戶合約收益（「國際財務報告
                in accordance with IFRS 15 Revenue from                                    準則第 15 號」）計量除外。直接歸屬
                Contracts with Customers (“IFRS 15”).                                      收購或發行金融資產及金融負債（按
                Transaction costs, including front-end fees and                            公平值計入損益（「按公平值計入損
                commitment fees, that are directly attributable                            益」）的 金 融 資 產 或 金 融 負 債 除 外）
                to the acquisition or issue of financial assets and                        的 交 易 成 本（包 括 借 貸 手 續 費 用 及
                financial liabilities (other than financial assets or                      承 諾 費 用）乃 自 於 首 次 確 認 時 在 金
                financial liabilities at fair value through profit or                      融資產或金融負債（倘適用）的公平
                loss (“FVTPL”)) are added to or deducted from                              值增加或減少。直接歸屬收購按公
                the fair value of the financial assets or financial                        平值計入損益的金融資產或金融負
                liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition.                       債的交易成本乃即時於損益確認。
                Transaction costs directly attributable to the

                liabilities at FVTPL are recognised immediately in
                profit or loss.

                       The effective interest method is a method of                          實際利率法是計算金融資產或金融
                       calculating the amortised cost of a financial                         負債的攤銷成本，及將利息收入及
                       asset or financial liability and of allocating                        利息開支在相關期間內分配的方法。
                       interest income and interest expense over the                         實際利率是透過金融資產或金融負
                       relevant period. The effective interest rate is the                   債的預期壽命，或（倘適用）一段較
                       rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash                     短時間，精確貼現估計未來現金收
                       receipts and payments (including all fees and                         入 及 付 款（包 括 屬 於 實 際 利 率 整 體
                       points paid or received that form an integral part                    部分的所有已付或已收費用及貼息、
                       of the effective interest rate, transaction costs                     交 易 成 本 及 其 他 溢 價 或 折 讓）至 於
                       and other premiums or discounts) through the                          初始確認時賬面淨值的利率。
                       expected life of the financial asset or financial
                       liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period,
                       to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.

                       Financial assets                                                      金融資產

                       All regular way purchases or sales of financial                       所有以常規方式購買或出售的金融
                       assets are recognised and derecognised on                             資產以交易日基準確認及解除確認。
                       a trade date basis. Regular way purchases or                          以常規方式購買或出售指當購買或
                       sales are purchases or sales of financial assets                      出售金融資產時，須按有關的市場
                       that require delivery of assets within the time                       規例或慣例設定的時限內交付有關
                       frame established by regulation or convention in                      資產。
                       the market place concerned.

                       All recognised financial assets are measured                          所有已確認的金融資產其後全部按
                       subsequently in their entirety at either amortised                    攤 銷 成 本 或 公 平 值（視 乎 金 融 資 產
                       cost or fair value, depending on the classification                   分類而定）計量。
                       of the financial assets.

      4    KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                              4   估計不明朗因素之主要來源

           In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which           在應用本集團之會計政策（如附註 3 所述）時，
           are described in Note 3, the Manager is required to make               管理人需就其他資料並沒有透露的資產及負債
           estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts                   的賬面值作出估計及假設。該等估計及相關假
           of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent from           設乃按過往經驗及其他視為相關因素作出。實
           other sources. The estimates and underlying assumptions                際結果可能與該等估計不同。
           are based on historical experience and other factors that
           are considered to be relevant. Actual results may differ from
           these estimates.

           The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on               本集團持續審閱估計數字及相關假設。倘修訂
           an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                會計估計數字僅影響某一期間，則於修訂有關
           recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if           估計數字之期間內確認修訂；倘修訂影響本期
           the revision affects only that period or in the period of the          及未來期間，則於作出修訂及未來期間均須確
           revision and future periods if the revision affects both current       認有關修訂。
           and future periods.

           In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,            在應用本集團會計政策的過程中，管理人認為，
           the Manager is of the opinion that there are no instances of           並不存在使用估計技術會對於綜合財務報表內
           use of estimation techniques which may have a significant              確認的款項產生重大影響的情況，惟下列各項
           effect on the amounts recognised in the consolidated                   除外：
           financial statements other than as follows:

           (a)   Valuation of investment properties                               (a)   投資物業估值

                 based on the valuation performed by independent                        進 行 的 估 值 按 公 平 值 列 賬。釐 定 公 平 值
                 qualified external valuer. The determination of the                    涉及若干市場狀況（載於附註 13）的假設。
                 fair value involves certain assumptions of market                      在 依 賴 估 值 報 告 時，管 理 人 已 行 使 其 判
                 conditions which are set out in Note 13. In relying                    斷，並 信 納 估 值 法 及 估 值 所 用 的 主 要 參
                 on the valuation report, the Manager has exercised                     數 能 反 映 當 前 市 況。管 理 人 認 為 本 集 團
                 their judgement and is satisfied that the method of                    投 資 物 業 的 估 值 為 最 佳 估 計。該 等 假 設
                 valuation and the key parameters used in the valuation                 變動會導致本集團投資物業公平值變動
                 are reflective of the current market conditions. The                   及綜合損益及其他全面收入表所呈報收
                 Manager considers the valuation of the Group’s                         益或虧損金額相應調整。
                 investment properties is the best estimate. Changes
                 to the assumptions would result in changes in the fair

    assess their performance.

    The Group owns 16 (2024:16) and 1 (2024: 1) investment                本集團分別擁有 16 項（2024 年：16 項）位於香

    reported to the Manager for the purposes of resource                  表現。管理人認為所有本集團現時持有的物業，
    allocation and performance assessment. The Manager                    包括零售商場，在向類似零售租戶出租以收取
    considers that all existing properties held by the Group,             租金收入的租賃服務上有相似的經濟特質。此
    consisting of retail shopping malls, have similar economic            外，它們經營的成本架構及經濟環境亦相似。

     The Group uses interest rate swaps as hedging instruments                         本集團利用利率掉期作為對沖工具，藉將銀行
     to manage its exposure to interest rate movements on                              借貸的一部分由浮動利率掉期至固定利率，以
     its bank borrowings by swapping a proportion of these                             管理該等借貸的利率變動風險。
     borrowings from floating rates to fixed rates.

     are complied with.

     50% (2024: 50%) of the gross asset value of the Group in               本 集 團 總 資 產 價 值 的 50%（ 2024 年：50% ）。
